## 🛡️ Verification Standard

Unlike auto-scraped directories, Toolvern enforces a strict verification protocol:
1. **Live Pricing Verification:** We do not guess or use memory. Pricing tables and annual discount terms are fetched directly from vendor pricing pages.
2. **No Hallucinated Capabilities:** We differentiate between standalone SaaS, wrapper APIs, and full enterprise AI suites.
3. **Link Integrity:** Direct links to official software pages, companion comparison breakdowns, and verified alternatives.
